`CreationProfileRequestV1` requires `CreationPreset.ExperiencedA0` and a closed `CreationOverridesV1`; the result is resolved and detached as `CreationProfileV3` before initialization. Only prior Neow bool, a complete three-row per-act boss-set override, and a typed stored Note descriptor are overrideable. The profile fixes standard, unmodded, non-Daily/non-trial, fully-unlocked Act4-enabled Ironclad A0. It has no character/ascension/mode/unlock/start-resource override and no `note_exchange_available`/`note_enabled` field. A0 non-Daily Note eligibility is an in-scope mechanic rule, not a user disable bit; event selection is not guaranteed.

| Accepted producer field/law | Exact consumer leaf(s) | Bound rule / checkpoint / open equality |
|---|---|---|
| PR27:A1C-D05/A1C-REQ-029 — standard_profile.character/rules | `RunState.profile.character; RunState.profile.ascension; RunState.profile.unlocks` | fixed standard unmodded non-Daily/non-trial fully-unlocked Act4-enabled Ironclad A0; character Ironclad, ascension 0, all five unlock sets all_unlocked=true with empty included/excluded deltas; no caller override for character/Ascension/mode/unlock |
| PR27:A1C-D05/A1C-REQ-029; E07 H1/H2 — prior Ironclad Neow eligibility | `RunState.profile.prior_neow_eligible` | independent bool, default true in ExperiencedA0; separate from unlocks and all boss-discovery sets; project RNG seed does not set original custom-seed mode or force eligibility |
| E07 H3/H5; PR33/PR36 accepted boss setup callers — ordinary bosses seen, by act | `RunState.profile.bosses_seen_by_act[].{act,bosses_seen}; RunState.history.boss_discoveries[] for current-run additions` | exact act rows 1/2/3; unique partial/empty sets legal; ExperiencedA0 contains all nine; source member names are semantic keys pending exact CAT literals; each discovery commits at actual prebattle hook, persists after death, never on announcement/selection/victory |
| E07 N1; PR27 A1C-REQ-029 — stored Note card ID and upgrade count | `RunState.profile.stored_note.{card_key,upgrade_count}; initial RunState.history.note.{stored_card,upgrades}` | ExperiencedA0 absent-preference default is exact ContentKey(Card,iron_wave)/0 from the accepted PR28 registry; explicit unknown key rejects, never silently falls back; complete Note-storable membership and per-card upgrade domain remain CAT/NUM joins |
| E07 N2 — incoming Note obtain/update ordering | `RunState.history.note plus EXEC-owned source-qualified continuations/checkpoints` | incoming obtain/hooks/master-deck notification precede outgoing selector construction; callback closure belongs to EXEC, not this creation consumer correction |
| E07 N3 — outgoing Note candidate restriction | `CreationProfileV3 stored_note admission via CAT Note-storable/permanent/removable predicate; deck instance identity remains PermanentCard.handle` | preserve legal off-color/later-origin stored cards; color/later origin alone are not exclusions; reject temporary-only, nonpurgeable and bottle-ineligible records by accepted content/card predicates; exact roster and property IDs remain CAT joins |
| E07 N4 — remove selected object then retain same live card | `RunState.history.note.pending_object:Option<PermanentCard>` | after object-removal callbacks, retain the same owned physical card with current live snapshot/growth until persistence; never replace it with a selection-time ID/count snapshot or reset live growth before removal hooks |
| E07 N5 — persist current ID/upgrades at nextRoomTransition | `RunState.history.note.pending_object → stored_card/upgrades; persistence_checkpoint` | sample retained live card at source-qualified next-room-transition checkpoint, commit one native in-memory replacement, clear pending object; not at Take/selection/event COMPLETE/map; source flush is not crash-atomicity guarantee |
| E07 N6 — A0 non-Daily Note availability | `standard A0 run rule; no profile field or override` | mechanic eligibility is inherent to in-scope A0 non-Daily rules; no user note_exchange_available/note_enabled switch; event selection is still not guaranteed |
| E07 N7 — Ignore branch | `RunState.history.note.pending_object=None and committed stored descriptor unchanged` | Ignore does not create an outgoing saved reference or a Note replacement merely because the event was visited |
| E07 H4 — Act1 boss prebattle discovery | `RunState.history.boss_discoveries[].{act,boss_key,checkpoint}` | append discovery at exact prebattle checkpoint even if later death; announcement, boss selection and victory are not discovery checkpoints; exact final EXEC SiteKey remains open |
| E07 H5 — Acts2/3 discovery history separation | `RunState.profile.bosses_seen_by_act[act=2/3]` | per-act rows, not one all_seen bool; Act3 history is retained in profile but Act3 gameplay is beyond this Act1→Act2Ready unit |

| Standard-start owner | Exact initialized consumer values |
|---|---|
| Profile | `RunState.profile` = resolved detached `CreationProfileV3`; not caller alias |
| Player | `RunState.player.character=Ironclad`; `RunState.player.ascension=0`; `RunState.player.hp=80`; `RunState.player.max_hp=80`; `RunState.player.gold=99` |
| Starting relic | one `RelicInstance` at `RunState.player.inventory.relics[]:RelicInstance`, semantic key `Burning Blood`, unique owned handle and matching `InventoryState.relic_order[]` reference; exact catalog literal remains open |
| Permanent starter deck | `RunState.player.deck.permanent_instances[]:PermanentCard`: 5 red Ironclad Strikes + 4 red Ironclad Defends + red Bash = 10 distinct permanent-card handles, each upgrade count 0; exact identity keys are closed by the accepted PR28 registry in §A.3; full property snapshots and later placement remain NUM/EXEC joins; no combat card is created merely by profile construction |
| Potions and keys | `RunState.player.inventory.potion_slots=[None,None,None]`; `RunState.player.inventory.keys` has Ruby/Sapphire/Emerald all false |
| Initial Note/history | `RunState.history.note` stores the resolved profile descriptor, with `exchange_used=false`, no persistence checkpoint and no pending object; `RunState.history.boss_discoveries=[]` |
| Identity allocator | every relic/card owns a unique same-run typed Handle; relic_order points to its owned RelicInstance; deck permanent instances each own one handle; no cross-episode handles or aliasing; allocate monotonically unique per-run ordinals only after whole-batch validation; exact allocation sequence is native construction order and does not claim source UUID parity; next_handle_ordinal is first unused ordinal |
| Explicit boundary | Only the accepted standard-start values above are fixed by this creation binding. Any other player/map/Neow/Act1Ready initialization values require their accepted producer joins; do not infer energy, stance, pile order, map or initial draw values here. |

**Defaults, validation, projection, reset and replay.** The named ExperiencedA0 default resolves all profile fields before initialization: prior Neow eligible, all three ordinary bosses seen in each act, and stored Note Iron Wave/0 only when no external saved preference is configured. Project RNG seed does not select original custom-seeded Neow semantics. Raw request shape/preset, every override row, exact per-act member domains, catalog Note-storage eligibility, and the actual per-card upgrade law are validated for the entire batch before seed/entropy, episode identity, slot, RNG cursor, elapsed time or reward changes. An unknown configured card key rejects; it never receives source's unknown-ID Iron Wave fallback. No generic upgrade cap is asserted. Off-color and later-origin Note cards remain admissible where the accepted Note-storage/removal predicates allow them; no color-only exclusion or arbitrary save import is added. The exact 370 accepted card identities/aliases and physical/control/current-scope distinctions are closed in §A.3; source route/pool/member equality, Note-storable membership, card properties, and upgrade laws remain CAT/NUM joins.

The captured profile and unoffered Note/boss identities live in CoreStateV9 and ReplayHeaderV7, not policy observations/metadata; Note identity/count become policy-visible only at the actual legal reveal, with no pre-reveal row/order/count leak. Each reset/vector replacement validates all rows first, captures a deep detached resolved profile, and initializes an independent RunState; no automatic cross-episode profile, Note or discovery inheritance. Replay stores the resolved profile plus `creation_profile_schema` and regenerates from start without rereading mutable callers/default preferences or restoring a snapshot. Core/replay equality includes initial owned RunState and later per-act history/Note changes. `HistoryState.note.pending_object` retains the same removed live card until the source-qualified next-room-transition persistence checkpoint, then commits its current key/count once; Ignore has no outgoing ref. Optional/staged/partial profile exports are not required. No historical `ProfileCandidate/ProfileStage/TimeState` subsystem is reintroduced.

**Still open after this consumer binding:** card acquisition route/pool/member equality, complete Note-storable catalog and per-card upgrade domains; remaining relic/boss literals/aliases and properties; full initial card numeric/property values; any other initial player/map/Neow/Act1Ready state; source-owned Note callbacks, exact persistence and boss discovery callback/checkpoint/site equality; Act2 successor/transition payload and hidden cutoff. The bounded 370-card identity/alias/classification registry is closed in §A.3. Act3 history is retained as profile input, but Act3 gameplay/caller sites are outside this Act1→Act2Ready unit.


#### Accepted PR28 card identity registry → consumer keys

The closed source set is accepted/reviewed PR28 branch head (routed bounded input) `7d81132bcdd02fccfcb51cb2affc28bba881b2fb`, `docs/research/2026-10-19-act1-complete-catalog-numeric.md`, SHA-256 `aaa72edab9f404a87bf227d0154a38c0c07961496dfc0e2cdbb5cb52ed176fb5`, §2.1 complete CardLibrary registration table plus its explicit identity/mechanics reconciliation. This appendix reproduces all 370 source rows and their exact key/alias/classification evidence so the registry is derivable without private files. Reconciliation is 370 loader identities = 364 in-scope physical mechanics identities + 3 nonphysical Wish callback controls + 3 registered physical current-scope exclusions; nine physical rows have multiple mechanics anchors. No `370=364` equivalence is asserted.

`ContentNamespace.Card=1` is the unchanged existing namespace code. A registry key `card.<suffix>` resolves exactly to `ContentKey(namespace=Card, semantic_key=<suffix>)`; `CardSnapshot.card_key:OwnerKey` uses the same existing `(ContentNamespace,AsciiKey)` wire fields. This card-key binding adds no field or namespace code; current core/replay versions are independently v9/v7 because of the later relic-history grammar correction. Registered catalog metadata is static validation input, not a run instance ID or public observation. Per-run instance handles remain distinct. Before the existing legal reveal checkpoint, no hidden key, alias, handle, or kind code may be projected.

Identity equality is byte-exact and bidirectional over canonical keys, exact source identities, exact class aliases (including absence), loader owner, mechanics anchors, and physical/control/current-scope classification. No trim, normalization, case fold, punctuation rewrite, alias fallback, or loader-order code assignment is allowed. A source-table em dash means no class alias is declared; the exact source identity remains independently retained and is not fabricated as an alias. Alias uniqueness is `(namespace, registry owner, exact alias)`; canonical keys and source identities are also unique. Registry lookup/codebook order is ascending unsigned UTF-8 bytes of complete canonical key. Exact table row references below are line anchors in the immutable pinned document.

**Closed registry / admission sets:** `AllRegistered = {r | r is one of the 370 exact PR28 §2.1 rows}`; `PhysicalCardSnapshot = {r ∈ AllRegistered | r.kind=physical-card ∧ r.scope=act1-physical}` (exactly 364); `WishControl = {r ∈ AllRegistered | r.kind=callback-control}` (exactly 3, never constructible as a physical/deck/combat card); `CurrentScopeExcluded = {r ∈ AllRegistered | r.kind=physical-card ∧ r.scope=registered-out-of-scope}` (exactly 3, rejected by this Act1 construction domain). Loader membership and source color are not acquisition-route predicates. The 364-key identity admission set is not a source candidate pool, complete Note-storable set, or upgrade-law domain.

Starter constructors resolve red Strike → `ContentKey(Card,strike_red)`, red Defend → `ContentKey(Card,defend_red)`, Bash → `ContentKey(Card,bash)` (5/4/1 copies respectively). ExperiencedA0's absent-preference Note preset resolves Iron Wave/0 → `ContentKey(Card,iron_wave)`. These close identity only; they do not close snapshot numeric/property fields, Note storage/removal membership, or upgrade laws.

**Generated-output identity bridge (accepted PR35, bounded to card-valued keys).** Every emitted physical card instance must resolve through the closed `PhysicalCardSnapshot` set above before admission; an unknown, Wish-control, or current-scope-excluded key rejects. Dynamic candidate/route/entitlement law remains the producer's domain and is not inferred from this identity set. Exact producer-member card identities are recorded only where a byte-exact source identity or class alias match exists; other producer-member joins remain explicit. Fixed Mark of Pain Wound outputs resolve to `ContentKey(Card,wound)`. Alchemize and Entropic Brew effect outputs are potion instances, not CardSnapshots; their own card-member identity is an independent catalog lookup.

**Identity validation controls:** a missing registered key, changed exact alias, duplicate alias, duplicate multi-anchor physical row, namespace drift, Wish-as-card, excluded-card admission, wrong starter/preset key, and unknown configured Note identity all reject before consumer mutation. Source color cannot stand in for acquisition-route membership. An unknown configured Note ID never receives Iron Wave fallback. The closed identity table does not assert full Note override eligibility or any per-card upgrade cap/law.

**Still open after this CAT identity unit:** exact source route/pool/entitlement joins for generated candidates; exact member-card mappings explicitly marked OPEN above; Note-storable/removable callback predicates; complete numeric/property domains and upgrade laws; non-card namespace joins; remaining CAT literal/member/alias/property equality, HAND initial-state/Act2Ready equality, and EXEC callback/site/checkpoint/settlement equality. No final catalog/core fingerprint or closure claim follows.


**Accepted PR35 play-origin binding (bounded static producer input, not final EXEC IDs).** Exact source fields in `OriginState`, `PlayCapture`, `UseDispatch`, `Payment`, and `HookEvent` map one-to-one to the concrete private-core leaves below. `PlayExecutionStateV1` is reachable through `RunState.play_executions`; its five typed groups preserve source record boundaries. All mapped fields are private causal state, not public observation columns; exact NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open. The accepted source is linked without copying at `docs/research/2026-10-19-act1-complete-play-origins.md`, main commit `d4046af1c8d0ad3c534454a8c18134f2c0432c7d`, SHA-256 `1b6fb3a6b524a18d693727279b6df660f90371fcda4c55c00207b4e2f1290658`. Source aliases and method locators are not EXEC wire IDs; every exact final EXEC site/action ID remains future/unresolved.

#### Source-field → concrete consumer-field map

Every source field and every leaf in the five consumer records occurs exactly once. Domain and checkpoint columns separate producer snapshots from later live values.

| Accepted source field | Concrete consumer field:type | Domain / meaning | Checkpoint | Visibility |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `OriginState.origin` | `PlayOriginStateV1.origin:AsciiKey` | exact origin IDs in origin_members; no EXEC numeric IDs assigned | source queue-origin assignment at actual play attempt | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `OriginState.physicalCardId` | `PlayOriginStateV1.physical_card_id:Handle` | unique live physical Card Handle | queue construction/actual physical instance resolution | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `OriginState.sourceCardId` | `PlayOriginStateV1.source_card_id:Option<Handle>` | Option<Handle>, exact producer instance or absent | producer copy/move provenance commit | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `OriginState.permanentCardId` | `PlayOriginStateV1.permanent_card_id:Option<OwnerKey>` | Option<OwnerKey>, permanent growth owner or absent | permanent deck owner resolution when one exists | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `OriginState.copyRelation` | `PlayOriginStateV1.copy_relation:PlayCopyRelation` | none\|same_uuid_distinct_object\|fresh_uuid\|moved_same_object | producer copy/move construction checkpoint | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `OriginState.producerSite` | `PlayOriginStateV1.producer_site:AsciiKey` | exact source class/member locator alias; EXEC site mapping unresolved | actual producer source member; final EXEC site registration unresolved | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `OriginState.playOrdinal` | `PlayOriginStateV1.play_ordinal:Option<U64>` | Option<U64>; absent for suppressed special dispatch without committed play history | history_commit; absent for suppressed special dispatch | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.energyOnUse` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.energy_on_use:I32` | signed I32; -1 is source X sentinel; -2 is unplayable, not X | producer queue construction/capture; sentinel retained | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.energyAtEnqueue` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.energy_at_enqueue:I32` | signed I32 captured at exact enqueue point | the exact queue constructor/action enqueue point for that origin | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.target` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.target:Option<Handle>` | Option<live Enemy Handle> | targetCapture boundary in same record; Omni dispatch-random is the sole exception | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.targetCapture` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.target_capture:PlayTargetCapture` | queue_construction\|producer_action_creation\|producer_action_execution\|producer_callback\|queue_dispatch\|direct_input\|none | one exact closed target-capture stage enum | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.randomTarget` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.random_target:Bool` | independent queue boolean | queue construction; independent of captured target randomness | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.immediateCard` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.immediate_card:Bool` | independent queue boolean | queue construction | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.autoplayCard` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.autoplay_card:Bool` | independent queue boolean | queue construction | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.isEndTurnAutoPlay` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.is_end_turn_auto_play:Bool` | independent queue boolean | queue construction | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.ignoreEnergyTotal` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.ignore_energy_total:Bool` | independent queue boolean | queue construction; not later ignoreEnergyOnUse | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.freeToPlayOnceAtCapture` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.free_to_play_once_at_capture:Bool` | bool snapshot, not later payment input | producer capture snapshot only | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.turnCostAtCapture` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.turn_cost_at_capture:I32` | signed live costForTurn snapshot, not later payment input | producer capture snapshot only; not payment input | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.purgeOnUse` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.purge_on_use:Bool` | independent bool | producer queue/copy construction | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.exhaustOnUse` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.exhaust_on_use:Bool` | independent queue request, not inherent exhaust | producer queue construction; not inherent exhaust | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.targetPolicy` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.target_policy:PlayTargetPolicy` | one of the nine accepted closed target-policy values | producer-origin exact closed target policy | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `PlayCapture.notificationMode` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.notification_mode:PlayNotificationMode` | normal\|suppressed_cleanup | normal manager entry versus explicit suppressed cleanup branch | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |

| `UseDispatch.turnCostAtPayment` | `UseDispatchStateV1.turn_cost_at_payment:I32` | signed live costForTurn read immediately before ordinary debit | after use/action-construction/other-card callbacks/hand removal, immediately before ordinary debit | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `UseDispatch.freeToPlayAtPayment` | `UseDispatchStateV1.free_to_play_at_payment:Bool` | live card.freeToPlay() result at ordinary payment checkpoint | same live payment lookup immediately before ordinary debit | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `UseDispatch.targetAtDispatch` | `UseDispatchStateV1.target_at_dispatch:Option<Handle>` | Option<Enemy Handle>; queued value except Omni one-time live dispatch random | before canUse only for Omni randomTarget; otherwise exact queued target | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `Payment.payer` | `PlayPaymentStateV1.payer:PlayPayer` | player\|none | named payment/no-payment branch | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `Payment.predicate` | `PlayPaymentStateV1.predicate:AsciiKey` | named exact ordinary/X/no-payment branch | named ordinary fixed/X/suppressed branch | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `Payment.basis` | `PlayPaymentStateV1.basis:PlayPaymentBasis` | turn_cost\|live_energy\|none\|card_local_x | named ordinary or card-local X payment branch | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `Payment.amount` | `PlayPaymentStateV1.amount:Option<I32>` | Option<I32>, exact committed or absent amount | at exact debit checkpoint; absent when no payment | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |
| `Payment.checkpoint` | `PlayPaymentStateV1.checkpoint:PlayPaymentCheckpoint` | manager_admission\|after_use_entry_callbacks_live_lookup\|x_action_resolution_live_lookup\|producer_effect\|none | source-defined payment checkpoint; ordinary and X are separate | private_core_only; no public observation carrier; later NUM/HAND/EXEC visibility joins remain open |

#### Copy-mutation receiver controls

| Exact producer member | Mutation receiver | Consumer provenance/state relation | Constructor/copy law |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Eviscerate` | `source_instance` | DeckState.generated_instances[].handle or PermanentCard.handle for the source; mutation provenance remains attached to source identity | source mutation applies to source before/through makeCopy; returned copy remains separate |
| `Blood for Blood` | `returned_fresh_copy` | DeckState.generated_instances[].handle for returned copy; source handle remains distinct | damagedThisCombat mutation targets returned fresh copy |
| `Searing Blow` | `constructed_output_instance` | DeckState.generated_instances[].snapshot.upgrade_count and copied physical Handle; source and output handles remain distinct | count-taking constructor does not replay prior damage upgrades; stat-equivalent construction has separate makeCopy/field restore path |

#### Checkpoint, action, copy, payment, and hook joins

| Source checkpoint | Exact consumer checkpoint/state |
|---|---|
| `queue_construction` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.target_capture / energy_on_use / energy_at_enqueue` |
| `producer_action_creation` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.target_capture and captured target/queue flags` |
| `producer_action_execution` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.target_capture and captured target/queue flags` |
| `producer_callback` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.target_capture; repeat uses parent UseCardAction.target and parent energyOnUse` |
| `queue_dispatch` | `UseDispatchStateV1.target_at_dispatch; Omni is the only named dispatch-random rule` |
| `direct_input` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.target_capture=direct_input and selected queue target` |
| `none` | `PlayCaptureStateV1.target_capture=none; null target remains null` |
| `admission` | `UseDispatchStateV1.free_to_play_once_at_admission; canUse precedes derived dispatch writes` |
| `history_commit` | `PlayHookEventStateV1(event=history,checkpoint=history_commit); late target invalidity does not rollback` |
| `use_entry` | `PlayHookEventStateV1(event=onUse/otherCardPlayed,checkpoint=use_entry)` |
| `after_use` | `PlayHookEventStateV1(event=onAfterUse,checkpoint=after_use), player power pass before monster power pass` |
| `cleanup` | `PlayHookEventStateV1(event=destination,checkpoint=cleanup); purge/power return differs from ordinary destination` |
| `manager_admission` | `PlayPaymentStateV1.checkpoint; no payment for earlier rejected admission` |
| `after_use_entry_callbacks_live_lookup` | `PlayPaymentStateV1.checkpoint and UseDispatchStateV1 live cost/free values; after card.use/action construction/other callbacks/hand removal` |
| `x_action_resolution_live_lookup` | `PlayPaymentStateV1.checkpoint; card-local X branch only on exact -1 sentinel and its own guard` |
| `producer_effect` | `PlayPaymentStateV1.checkpoint; named producer-specific payment branch` |

**Closed consumer payment predicate keys:** `ordinary_fixed_cost_debit`; `autoplay_waiver`; `free_flag_waiver`; `corruption_skill_waiver`; `nonpositive_cost_no_debit`; `card_local_x_debit`; `x_nonpositive_no_debit`; `x_no_orb_no_effect_or_debit`; `suppressed_end_turn_no_debit`; `no_payment`.

**Closed hook recipient-order keys:** `powers`; `relics`; `stance`; `blights`; `hand`; `discard`; `draw`; `player_powers`; `monster_powers`. The actual per-event recipient sequence is recorded in `HookEvent.recipientOrder`; EXEC still owns exact callback/owner sequence equality.

#### Accepted X-action/payment submatrix

| Exact X-card member | Effect/capture law | Payment guard/basis | Zero-count debit | Orb gate |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Whirlwind` | captured energyOnUse; -1 falls back to live current energy at action; Chemical X +2 once | positive adjusted count and live freeToPlayOnce false; basis `live_energy` | no | none |
| `Skewer` | captured energyOnUse; -1 falls back to live current energy at action; Chemical X +2 once | positive adjusted count and live freeToPlayOnce false; basis `live_energy` | no | none |
| `Reinforced Body` | captured energyOnUse; -1 falls back to live current energy at action; Chemical X +2 once | positive adjusted count and live freeToPlayOnce false; basis `live_energy` | no | none |
| `Malaise` | captured energyOnUse; -1 falls back to live current energy; Chemical X +2 once | positive adjusted count and live freeToPlayOnce false; basis `live_energy` | no | none |
| `Doppelganger` | captured energyOnUse; -1 falls back to live current energy; Chemical X +2 once | positive adjusted count and live freeToPlayOnce false; basis `live_energy` | no | none |
| `Tempest` | captured energyOnUse; -1 falls back to live current energy; Chemical X +2 once | positive adjusted count and live freeToPlayOnce false; basis `live_energy` | no | none |
| `Collect` | captured energyOnUse; -1 falls back to live current energy; Chemical X +2 once | positive adjusted count and live freeToPlayOnce false; basis `live_energy` | no | none |
| `Multi-Cast` | captured energyOnUse; -1 falls back to live current energy; Chemical X +2 once | current orb required for both effect and payment; live freeToPlayOnce read at payment; basis `live_energy` | no | required |
| `Transmutation` | use raises captured energy to at least current energy; action fallback and Chemical X apply; output per effective positive X | positive output count and live freeToPlayOnce false; basis `live_energy` | no | none |
| `Conjure Blade` | upgraded producer forwards capture+1; fallback only for exactly -1; Chemical X +2; Expunger created at zero | live freeToPlayOnce false; payment outside positive-count condition; basis `live_energy` | yes | none |

#### PLAY X-payment outcomes → persistent continuation payment owner

The frozen recheck-v4 I1 is addressed on its corrected basis. Accepted PR35 §3.2 says ordinary fixed-cost debit and card-local X payment are separate checkpoints and must never both be charged. The previous ordinary-plus-X Whirlwind witness is withdrawn: no accepted legal positive-cost X writer/witness is claimed. The gap was the missing exact X-outcome-to-persistent-owner route, not a claim that the generic grammar could not represent multiple outcomes. No payment vector is added: each actual X action invocation owns its existing `ContinuationState.payment` under `RunState.continuations`.

`ContinuationState.originating_play:Option<PlayActionRef>` is the stable tuple `(physical_card_id:Handle, play_ordinal:U64, action_invocation:U64)`, never continuation allocation order or a future EXEC id. Existing `parent/children` retain continuation graph links. `source`/`phase.source` are typed `SiteKey`; `resume` is a `CheckpointKey`; the payment variant's checkpoint and commit key retain the exact resolution boundary. For a payment-bearing continuation, `PlayActionRef.action_invocation == ContinuationState.resume.ordinal == payment checkpoint ordinal == CommitKey.invocation`; `CommitKey.source_ordinal` distinguishes any separate outcome within that invocation. `CommittedPayment` stores amount/resource/`CommitKey`; its key occurs exactly once in the continuation's `commit_keys` and run `commit_ledger`. Payment continuations and their play reference/graph/checkpoint state persist through causal settlement in CoreStateV9 and from-start replay equality; resume of an already committed key never debits again.

| Continuation payment state | Exact distinction |
|---|---|
| `NotDue` | payment checkpoint has not yet been reached; not equivalent to resolved no-debit |
| `NoPayment` | checkpoint was reached and accepted guard/amount/resource law resolved to no debit; NoPaymentOutcome retains exact checkpoint and closed predicate key |
| `Reserved` | debit is due/reserved but not yet committed; preserve exact amount/resource/checkpoint |
| `Committed` | debit committed exactly once; retain amount/resource/CommitKey in continuation and commit ledger; resume never reapplies |
| `Refused` | attempted payment was explicitly refused; preserve reason, distinct from NoPayment and NotDue |
| `Cancelled` | payment reservation was explicitly cancelled; preserve disposition, do not erase a prior committed key |

Ordinary outcome remains solely `RunState.play_executions[].payment:Option<PlayPaymentStateV1>` and may be absent or uncommitted independently. X outcomes do not overwrite it; distinct action continuations do not authorize repeating a debit. `NotDue` means its checkpoint has not run; `NoPayment` means it ran and resolved to no debit; committed, reserved, refused, and cancelled outcomes remain distinct.

**Ordinary fixed-cost predicate:** `live costForTurn > 0 AND NOT live card.freeToPlay() AND NOT isInAutoplay AND NOT (Corruption AND Skill); amount is then-current live costForTurn`.

`ActionKind.PlayCard` is the actual consumer action enum member, not a final EXEC wire ID. Ordinary payment uses live `costForTurn` and `freeToPlay()` only after card use, UseCardAction construction, other-card callbacks and hand removal. Captured `turnCostAtCapture` and queue `ignoreEnergyTotal` are never substituted for those reads. X branches use the named local action checkpoint only for the exact -1 sentinel; Conjure Blade can pay at zero output, while Multi-Cast requires a current orb. Copy receivers are source-qualified: Eviscerate mutates the source instance, Blood for Blood the returned fresh copy; Searing Blow's count-taking constructor does not replay prior damage upgrades. Hooks preserve event order and exact recipient pass. These are accepted static producer facts and authored consumer bindings, not source/runtime equality or gameplay results.

A producer import may fill a declared `OwnerKey`, `ContentKey`, site owner/operation, enum member name, source-qualified numeric value, or source-specific legal domain. It may not change a record's field set/order, primitive width, option/reference shape, tagged union structure, canonical codec, ownership resolution rule, or lifetime boundary without a new consumer schema version. For source-derived variable keys the key string set is open pending the named import; each actual key is still strict nonempty ASCII and each mapping is explicit and unique.

### A.4 Consumer wire-code maps

Every pair below is `member=U32 wire code`; values are frozen consumer assignments, not source enum ordinals. A producer's member/key set is reconciled separately and cannot renumber these codes. ## Potion consumer binding (accepted finite producer)

The potion row is an observation of the existing `InventoryState.potion_slots` owner,
not a second potion inventory. Each actual slot ordinal is emitted, including an empty
slot. An empty slot has `occupied=false`, `potion_code=absent`, and no potency; it is
not a fabricated zero-potency potion. An occupied row has a run-local `PotionInstance`
handle, the namespace-qualified `OwnerKey(Potion, exact factory ID)` below, its actual
slot, and signed-i32 captured potency. The source factory ID, stable content key,
instance handle, and belt ordinal are distinct identities. The source ID→key mapping is
byte-exact (no display-name normalization); unknown IDs reject. The held instance and
its slot are public; potency is public only for an occupied held instance. Unheld
offers/proposals are represented by their offer/continuation owner and do not create a
row in table 6 until obtained.

These 33 rows are the selected Ironclad `PotionHelper.getPotions(IRONCLAD,false)`
factory identities; they are not a claim that other character-exclusive or special
non-factory classes belong to this set. Shared capture uses the current ascension
accessor and optional Sacred Bark doubling with signed-i32 wrap; later use consumes
the stored instance value rather than recomputing it. Concrete zero/no-amount cases,
including Smoke Bomb, remain zero-valued and are not converted into a fictitious
potency effect. The four native properties for a potion row bind as follows:
`belt_slot` is the actual list ordinal (source signed-int represented by the declared
slot i32 domain); `occupied` is derived from whether that list element is a placeholder;
`potion_code` is the byte-exact source alias lookup above; and `potency` is the stored
signed-i32 instance member. Table 6's slot ordinals are the only public references
needed for potion use/discard actions; there is no need to publish private source
flags, description, tips, target-required flag, or discarded/thrown bookkeeping.

Lifecycle binding is owner/checkpoint-specific. Ordinary acquisition scans the live
belt in order, replaces the first placeholder and sets the instance slot/obtained
state; a full belt refuses acquisition. The indexed acquisition overload is not that
scan. A reward claim checks Sozu before acquisition: Sozu may remove the visible offer
without creating ownership, while a non-Sozu full-belt claim retains it. Shop offer
visibility remains independent and Sozu blocks purchase at its own route. Combat
`ObtainPotionAction` and outside-combat `ObtainPotionEffect` perform separate Sozu and
capacity checks when their queued proposal resolves; a proposal is not an owned
instance and failed acquisition does not resample. Manual use validates `canUse`,
dispatches the source effect, runs ordered `onUsePotion` relic hooks, then replaces
the held slot with its same-ordinal placeholder. For targeted use, cancel only exits
target mode: it does not dispatch, run hooks, or consume. Discard similarly replaces
the slot but does not invoke use or hooks; We Meet Again disallows it. Sozu does not
prohibit use or discard of held potions. Fairy Potion is the exception to manual use:
its manual `canUse` is always false, while qualifying lethal damage automatically
rescues through the damage path, heals, and removes the potion without manual-use hooks.
`makeCopy` creates a fresh same-class instance, not a clone of slot/potency/lifecycle
fields. These are the accepted bounded branches; they do not assert whole-run reset
callsite closure or source-frame timing.

Concrete join checks: `Fire Potion` is an occupied source instance with its own slot
and stored potency, and its target-use action references that slot, not the factory
ID as an instance handle. A canceled target selection leaves that same occupied row
unchanged. A `PotionSlot(2)` projects slot 2 as unoccupied with absent ID/potency, not
three default potion records. A Sozu-blocked reward claim can remove its offered
reward without adding a table-6 row. Fairy automatic rescue removes the held Fairy row
without inventing a manual `UsePotion` commit. These distinctions are registry and
semantic bindings to existing types/properties/operations, not a wire change: no record,
field, enum, descriptor, version, canonical-byte, or standalone combat-v1 pin changes.
The exact receiver/sink/effect branches are bound only to the accepted potion producer;
downstream action IDs, other numeric families, reward catalog routes, and global reset
closure remain separate pending joins.

### A.5 Canonical descriptor and manifest bytes

The descriptor is the exact `Descriptor` record above; its object keys are exactly its declared field names, recursively. Unknown or missing fields, duplicate JSON keys, duplicate IDs/names/codes, invalid nested record fields, unknown enum codes/tags, and out-of-order declared arrays reject. JSON object keys are sorted by unsigned ASCII byte order. Arrays are never reordered by the serializer: fixed rows follow the schema list, tables by ascending table ID, each table/fixed-row columns by contiguous ordinal from zero, codebooks by name bytes and members by name bytes, references/visibility by field bytes, ordering by table bytes, actions/parameters by numeric code, compatibility and component hashes by name bytes. Arrays whose keys repeat are invalid. The serializer emits no whitespace; only null, booleans, signed-i64 integers, strings, arrays, and objects are permitted; floats and integers outside `[-2^63,2^63-1]` reject. Integer form is shortest base-10, no plus, leading zero, or negative zero. Strings are Unicode scalar sequences encoded as UTF-8; quote and backslash are escaped as `\"` and `\\`; every U+0000..U+001F is `\u00xx` with lowercase hex; every other scalar is emitted directly as UTF-8, with no normalization. Parser input is accepted only if strict UTF-8 JSON parses without duplicate keys and byte-for-byte reserialization equals the original bytes. This descriptor/manifest JSON grammar is distinct from the action-JSON grammar in §10; action JSON rules do not implicitly define descriptor bytes.

A production descriptor additionally requires fixed rows exactly `context` (12 ordered columns) then `player` (16); table IDs exactly 1..18 with widths `[7,3,9,12,4,4,8,5,5,9,7,5,6,8,7,8,6,5]` (118 table columns total); one visibility record for every fully qualified fixed/table carrier field; every `ActionKind` and `ParameterKind` code/name pair; the exact complete `enums` codebook set with wire width 4 and exact member/code pairs in this appendix; the exact `union/<name>` codebook set with wire width 2 and lexicographically derived tag/code pairs; and exactly 10 component hashes. An empty descriptor is only a serializer test vector and fails production-descriptor validation. This shape gate does not close any producer-supplied member/domain/visibility/equality join.

Component bytes are themselves canonical encodings of their versioned closed component records. For component `(name,schema_id,bytes)`, `component_hash = SHA256(ASCII("spire-lab-act1-descriptor-component-v1") || 00 || U64BE(len(UTF8(schema_id))) || UTF8(schema_id) || U64BE(len(bytes)) || bytes)`. Descriptor `component_hashes` contains the exact lowercase 64-hex digest and schema ID for each component, sorted by ASCII name; the set is exactly `{observation, action, content-site, visibility, numeric-fields, native-rng, private-core, replay, task, delivery}` once all inputs close. While any import is unresolved, no final descriptor, component, or composed fingerprint is claimed; a structural test vector below is not a production descriptor.

For canonical descriptor byte string `D`, `descriptor_preimage = ASCII("spire-lab-act1-descriptor-v7") || 00 || U64BE(len(D)) || D`, and `descriptor_digest = SHA256(descriptor_preimage)`. No self-hash is included: component hashes are computed first, inserted into D, then D is hashed. A change to a serialized observation field/codebook/visibility rule requires an observation descriptor version change. Core/replay-only serialized changes use their own versioned schema references and change the composed descriptor digest without needlessly advancing the unchanged observation wire version. This is a precise preimage rule, not a claimed final hash.

#### Canonical vectors

- For input object `{"b":2,"a":"é"}`, canonical bytes are hex `7b2261223a22c3a9222c2262223a327d` (UTF-8 for `{"a":"é","b":2}`).
- String-escape vector for value containing quote, backslash, LF, TAB, slash, and `é` has canonical bytes `7b2273223a225c225c5c5c75303030615c75303030392fc3a9227d`; controls use lowercase `\u00xx`, while slash and non-ASCII scalar bytes remain literal.
- Wire-code checks: `SiteNamespace.Run=6`, `RoomObjectKind.Chest=4`, `ActionKind.SourceOption=21`; `CommittedEffect.Card` is the lexicographic tag code shown by its `union/CommittedEffect` codebook.
- Component hash check: schema ID `schema.v1` and component bytes `{}` yield `0fbaea9ccdb4ed8fbf9b55b411b2b9f9e4218988a4c79644d5a90eb9a83f3320`.
- Empty structural candidate-7 observation descriptor vector uses core candidate 9 / replay 7 schema references in the corrected v12 vector; its canonical bytes therefore differ from the historical candidate-8/replay-6 vector. It is only a serializer vector: an empty table set is not a valid production Act 1 observation descriptor.
- RowRef vectors: absent `(0,-1)`; first row `(1,0)` with count 1; last representable slot row `(18,I64_MAX-1)` with count I64_MAX; reject `(0,-2)`, `(1,-1)`, unknown table 19, row equal to count, and segment count I64_MAX+1.

### A.6 Recursive grammar validation and boundary

The private correction validator resolves every referenced name through the finite primitive/enum/record/union registry, checks each record's complete ordered field manifest, every union's complete required variant manifest and every enum's frozen name/code manifest, rejects duplicate/malformed enum names/codes and validates nested values recursively. The public record tables above are the normative full expansion; no `Opaque`, arbitrary byte payload, untyped dictionary, missing-field fallback, or producer-defined structure is permitted. Producer-provided *values* and source-member sets remain open only at the explicitly identified joins in §11. Private negative controls omit nested `CheckpointKey.ordinal`, remove a required record field or `CommittedEffect.Resource` variant, mutate a frozen enum code, use an unknown nested union tag, and feed noncanonical descriptor bytes; each must fail. These structural controls establish only contract consistency, not runtime codec, source equality, or gameplay behavior.

### Potion lifecycle applicability and native projection (v10)

The following finite applicability record joins the native consumer grammar to the independently accepted numeric-members producer at commit `7667d9e6290db06943eeffb3848f2bc11431e829`, document SHA-256 `ab0a11467345b896e503ad9a76e9968989d27e558628bc2279ed2220c8fcf650`. `L01`–`L12` names, source-member/guard/outcome/lifetime records, and every cell are the producer's exact accepted family contracts; this matrix is not a new source audit. The 33×12 matrix therefore has exactly 396 owner-family cells; no source potion outside this accepted owner set is implied.

| Family | Source member(s) | Source guard and state | Source operation / outcome | Source visibility / lifetime | Native owner and field projection | Native guard / commit projection | Native outcome and lifetime |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| L01 | AbstractPotion.getPotency();AbstractPotion.initializeData();SacredBark.onEquip() | No-arg accessor reads current ascension and checks live player Bark; base initializeData is a no-op; fields: potency,description,tips; Bark reads player.potions | getPotency(int) then Bark signed-int doubling; initializeData writes captured potency/description/tips; Bark onEquip iterates current held entries and calls initializeData; result: base no-op; Bark refreshes existing entries without obtaining/removing/replacing slots | accessor is live; captured instance fields change only on initializeData refresh; no offer/belt visibility effect | RunState.player.inventory.potion_slots[*].PotionInstance; occupied table-6 row: potion_code + potency:i32 | initializeData/accessor refresh; no acquisition/removal commit | captured potency changes only at initialization refresh; no offer visibility |
| L02 | AbstractPlayer.obtainPotion(AbstractPotion);AbstractPlayer.obtainPotion(int,AbstractPotion) | ordinary path scans actual list ascending for first PotionSlot; indexed path returns only if slot > potionSlots; fields: potions,slot,isObtained | scan → first placeholder → setAsObtained(slot) → replace → flash/sound; indexed guard → ArrayList.set(index) → setAsObtained(index); result: ordinary true on first free placeholder, false when full; indexed void, upper-guard no-op only; equality/negative are not guarded before list access | acquisition writes occupied entry; indexed path is not a scan and neither method controls reward/offer visibility | InventoryState.potion_slots; table-6 belt_slot,occupied,potion_code,potency | scan chooses first placeholder; indexed overload remains separate; acquisition writes slot/obtained | owned row begins at replacement; full scan false; no offer mutation |
| L03 | RewardItem.claimReward() | potion reward switch arm; Sozu tested before obtain; fields: RewardItem.potion, player relic, reward completion/result | Sozu check → flash → return true; else obtainPotion scan → return its success result; result: Sozu true/removal without obtain; full belt false and reward retained; successful obtain true | claim-time checkpoint only; known reward stays visible until claim; not a shop/appearance rule | RunState.room.offers:Vec<Offer>; Offer.status, source_instance, claim_commit; InventoryState only after obtain; table-10 Offer and table-6 only on ownership | ClaimReward source commit; Sozu-before-obtain branch; claim commit may remove offer without slot write | claim checkpoint; full-belt refusal retains offer; Sozu success has no held row |
| L04 | ObtainPotionAction.update() | when duration reaches ACTION_DUR_XFAST; Sozu checked at update only; fields: queued potion, player relic, live belt capacity | duration guard → Sozu check/flash → (if absent) obtainPotion → tickDuration; result: Sozu skips obtain; no Sozu attempts current obtain; current capacity may fail; no resampling | action is queued before checkpoint; held-potion use and offer visibility are separate | queued obtain continuation/action; InventoryState at resolve; ContinuationState source/resume/commit_keys as required by generic continuation grammar; table-6 only after successful current obtain | duration guard then update-time Sozu/capacity; no resampling; no invented potion EXEC/site key | proposal precedes update; held row only after successful slot replacement |
| L05 | ObtainPotionEffect.update() | outside effect update; Sozu gates obtain; fields: queued potion, player relic, live belt, isDone | Sozu check → (if absent) obtainPotion → isDone=true; result: Sozu skips obtain; full belt may refuse; effect is done in either branch | delayed outside-combat effect checkpoint; distinct from reward claim/combat action | outside obtain effect continuation; InventoryState at resolve; ContinuationState source/resume/commit_keys as required by generic continuation grammar; table-6 only after successful current obtain | outside effect checks Sozu then obtains; completion independent of success; no invented potion EXEC/site key | effect completion is not ownership; full/Sozu refusal has no slot row |
| L06 | PotionPopUp.updateInput() | top click/select; canUse true; targetRequired false; fields: potion.canUse,targetRequired,slot,ordered player.relics,metrics | canUse → potion.use(null) → relic onUsePotion iteration → destroyPotion(slot) → close; result: valid use runs once then consumes slot; invalid use does not dispatch; Sozu is not a manual-use prohibition | held slot exists through use and ordered hooks, then becomes placeholder; no offer visibility change | InventoryState.potion_slots[slot] + ordered relic instances; UsePotion source is actual slot; target/choice only from legal menu | canUse then use(null), ordered hooks, destroy same slot; semantic UsePotion commit once | occupied row through use/hooks; same slot placeholder after consume |
| L07 | PotionPopUp.updateTargetMode();PotionPopUp.updateInput() | target mode accepts hovered live target; cancel input exits without selecting; fields: targetMode,hoveredMonster,potion,slot,ordered relic list | select → use(target) → combat HandCheckAction → relic hooks → destroy slot; cancel → clear target mode/cursor only; result: target selection consumes; cancel preserves potion and runs no use/hooks/destruction | targeted route applies only to FirePotion, WeakenPotion, FearPotion; 30 owners take nontarget route | InventoryState.potion_slots[slot] + current live target; UsePotion references actual slot and validated target; cancel carries no target commit | only targetRequired owners; accepted live target commits use; cancel clears target mode only | cancel preserves same occupied row and invokes no use/hooks/destroy |
| L08 | AbstractPotion.canDiscard();PotionPopUp.updateInput();TopPanel.destroyPotion(int) | bottom discard requires canDiscard; We Meet Again denies; fields: event,canDiscard,potion.slot,player.potions | canDiscard → destroyPotion(slot) → PotionSlot(slot) replacement; no use/onUsePotion on discard branch; result: blocked discard preserves item; permitted discard removes without payload/hooks; Sozu does not block discard | placeholder preserves ordinal; discard is not use or acquisition and does not change offer visibility | InventoryState.potion_slots[slot]; DiscardPotion source actual slot; no target/payload | canDiscard guard then destroy/reinsert PotionSlot(slot); no UsePotion/onUsePotion commit | blocked leaves row; permitted discard creates empty same ordinal |
| L09 | AbstractPotion.canUse();BloodPotion.canUse();FruitJuice.canUse();EntropicBrew.canUse();FairyPotion.canUse();SmokeBomb.canUse() | inherited gate checks event/WeMeetAgain, monsters, ended turn, combat; five concrete overrides checked individually; fields: room.phase,event,monster group/health,turnHasEnded; Smoke monster powers/type | popup calls canUse before dispatch; overrides replace or extend inherited gate; result: inherited false for blocked states; Blood/Fruit/Brew allow outside-combat subject to turn/event; Fairy always false; Smoke adds BackAttack/boss blocks | manual availability only; does not define reward/shop visibility or automatic Fairy rescue | PotionInstance.canUse inputs: room/event/combat/player/monster state; visibility is current legal manual action, not a new table-6 property | owner-specific canUse guard before UsePotion; Fairy always false; Smoke extra guards | gate applies to manual dispatch only; does not hide held instance or alter offers |

For every owner-family cell, the owner is `InventoryState.potion_slots[slot]` only where an occupied/slot instance exists; the branch's guard/result above controls whether the operation mutates that row. Empty `PotionSlot(slot)` projects `belt_slot=slot`, `occupied=false`, absent `potion_code`, and no potency. An offer or queued proposal is not owned and cannot create this row. Slot-consuming use/discard replaces only the source ordinal; the source does not license padding, transferring an offer into inventory before successful obtain, or serializing private source flags.

Owner distinctions enforced by the matrix: only Fire Potion, Weak Potion, and Fear Potion take the `L07` targeted route; Blood, Fruit Juice, Entropic Brew, Fairy, and Smoke Bomb take their exact `L09` override branch; only Entropic Brew has `L10`; only Fairy Potion has automatic `L11` rescue. A target-cancel has no use commit and preserves the row; permitted discard has a `DiscardPotion` slot mutation but no use/hook commit; Fairy rescue is damage-owned, not manual use; `L12` creates a fresh instance and is not a whole-run reset writer.

The source family records bind to existing table-6 slot/potion fields, table-10 offer status, typed `UsePotion`/`DiscardPotion` action records, and existing continuation/commit grammar. No potion-specific action/site/EXEC integer is introduced. This v10 join does not claim the unresolved downstream effect action IDs or global run-reset callsites.

### Relic identity, pool and caller consumer (v11)

This finite consumer join uses accepted handoff-profile commit `ff568b7c8d49ab47340f9216f4d0fd8064cf31a4`, document SHA-256 `28259aada5ec45d00692a9e0bff2e1a805f614af461901f0c5b6090a9cca5dea` and the accepted RNG/map registry on main `fe18c4155496da6f54c8335d9de174f1f6265936` (RNG/map document SHA-256 `1fa042e4bd68e8d799893fb61c9f614115167543ca1cfde5aae01b1103b93a7c`). The consumer input is immutable; these rows bind its already-reviewed identities, aliases, role sets, and caller records without re-extracting source.

The table is the literal accepted 141-row catalog, not a count-derived range. `random_pool_member` is the one exact tier role for the five random pools, or `none` for Starter/Special. The Ironclad-red overlay column is registration metadata over the existing ordinary member; it adds no pool row. IDs, keys, and acquired instance handles remain distinct.

#### Exact native owner, visibility, ordering, and lifecycle

`PlayerState.inventory.relics` owns acquired `RelicInstance(handle,relic_key,counter,used,properties)` records. `EconomyState.relic_pool` holds ordered `PoolEntry(key,eligible,consumed,ordinal)` entries whose exact identity keys resolve through the registry above; `HistoryState.relics` is the role-keyed five-element `Vec<RelicHistory>` defined in v12 below; the prior singular cardinality wording is superseded. The exact role qualifier is the `RelicHistory.pool` key listed in the role-key table; each `RelicHistory.remaining_keys` vector is that role's ordered selectable membership, cross-checked against the pool-entry vector and closed source-ID→role registry. Rejected selections append only to `RelicHistory.consumed_rejected`; generated offers add their content key to the existing `EconomyState.consumed_ids` and remove it from that role's remaining keys, whether later taken or skipped. `owned_exclusions` remain independent from both depletion forms. Each relic key is `OwnerKey(Relic,<exact constructor ID>)`. This binds existing `RelicInstance.relic_key`, table 5 `relic_code/public_position/counter/used`, `Offer.content/source_instance/status/claim_commit`, `EconomyState.consumed_ids`, and typed `OfferMutation`/`CommitKey` fields; it introduces no relic record or ordinal codebook.

On initialization, privately order the canonical full list for each of Common, Uncommon, Rare, Shop, and Boss, then remove owned and carried-consumed IDs while preserving each surviving order. Keep `owned_exclusions` distinct from `consumed_rejected`; never globally delete an ID across roles. A selected candidate is removed from that role and committed to its consumed history when its offer/stock row is generated—not when the offer is taken. Rejected candidates are also removed/recorded. Claim/take installs a separate owned handle; skip/no-pick/decline leaves that pool consumption intact and changes only the owning offer/claim state. The five role-ordered remaining-key vectors, flat pool entries, rejection ledgers, acquired instances and carried Ironclad registration state remain private; public table 5 reveals only acquired relic identity/order and each counter/used value under that relic's exact entitlement predicate. Current generated offers appear only under the offer row's legal visibility; never expose remaining pool IDs, pending candidate order, rejection ledger, hidden offers, or future generated identity. Static Starter/Special role identities are not inserted into random arrays; special outputs appear only at the source route, and fallback keys are not role members.

Normal selection consumes the role front; end selection consumes the role back, except Boss consumes the front for both helpers. Rejection consumes the rejected member and records it in that role's rejected history. A `canSpawn=false` rejection from normal resumes end selection at the originally requested role; an end rejection retries end at its requested role. Depleted Common promotes to Uncommon, Uncommon to Rare, Shop to Uncommon, via normal selection (also after end depletion). Empty Rare returns `Circlet`; empty Boss returns `Red Circlet`. Merchant rows 0/1 use end selection; row 2 is a fixed Shop-role selection and uses the same end/back rule. On Act1→Act2 carry, preserve exact remaining order, `consumed_ids`, and every per-role rejection/owned-exclusion history unchanged—no reinitialization, reshuffle, or reset.

#### Accepted caller-family → native consumer matrix

Each row is the exact immutable accepted caller family/checkpoint. Source tier selector and filter/result clause are copied from the accepted record, then paired with its existing native owner projection. Exact final EXEC/action/site/callback IDs remain pending and are not invented here.

| Accepted source/caller family | Source requested tier/selection | Source filter / result commit | Native pool/selector projection | Native offer/ownership/history commit |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Elite reward base relic | Common/Uncommon/Rare from `0..99`: 0–49/50–82/83–99; normal | Live `canSpawn`; consumed rejection/depletion and skip rules above. | Common/Uncommon/Rare; normal: front | live canSpawn; rejected candidates consumed; offer selection consumes before take/skip; relic_pool[role] + RelicHistory(role); Offer.content=Relic key; skip does not restore |
| Ordinary chest base relic | Size-conditioned shared chest roll: Small 0–49 Common+gold, 50–74 Common/no gold, 75–99 Uncommon; Medium 0–34 Common+gold, 35–84 Uncommon, 85–99 Rare; Large 0–49 Uncommon+gold, 50–74 Uncommon, 75–99 Rare; normal | After ordered chest-open hooks, before chest-open-after hooks. Sapphire link applies only to this base row. | Common/Uncommon/Rare; normal: front | size roll and sapphire only for base row; ordered chest-open hooks before/after; one role entry consumed at generated offer; chest's offer/claim state is distinct from pool state |
| Matryoshka extra nonboss chest relic | While counter>0, decrement first; strict Boolean 3/4 selects Common else Uncommon; normal | Separate extra offer/pool mutation from chest base and sapphire linkage; mark used at counter zero. | Common/Uncommon; normal: front | counter>0; decrement before strict Boolean 3/4 tier choice; separate pool mutation/offer from chest base and Sapphire; counter used at zero |
| Boss reward / Neow boss swap | Fixed Boss; normal | Boss front even if end-helper retry; Neow removes Burning Blood before candidate testing. | Boss; normal: front | Neow removes owned Burning Blood before candidate testing; Boss ignores end-selector reversal; Boss role entry consumed on generated offer; acquired relic instance is separate |
| Neow category 1/2 relic reward | Fixed Common / fixed Rare respectively; normal | No tier roll; live predicate; skip keeps consumed member. | Common / Rare; normal: front | fixed Common/Rare; live owner predicate; one generated offer consumes member even when skipped |
| Campfire Dig (Shovel) | Common/Uncommon/Rare from 0–49/50–82/83–99; normal | One offer; no chest/key link; claim or skip after selection. | Common/Uncommon/Rare; normal: front | tier roll; no chest/key link; single generated offer; claim/skip does not restore role member |
| Black Star extra reward | Common/Uncommon/Rare from 0–49/50–82/83–99; normal noncampfire | Only actual `MonsterRoomElite`, not EventRoom `eliteTrigger`; additionally consume/reject Peace Pipe, Shovel, Girya. | Common/Uncommon/Rare; normal noncampfire: front | actual MonsterRoomElite only; additionally reject Peace Pipe/Shovel/Girya; role consumption and owned acquisition are distinct; generated offer persists through skip |
| Big Fish Box, Dead Adventurer successful-search relic token, Scrap Ooze, Wheel of Change relic result, We Meet Again sacrifice | Each source's own 0–99 tier draw Common 0–49, Uncommon 50–82, Rare 83–99; screenless normal | Consume/reject Bottled Flame, Bottled Lightning, Bottled Tornado, Whetstone and continue requested tier. Dead Adventurer consumes its once-shuffled GOLD/NOTHING/RELIC token before this; no token reroll. | Common/Uncommon/Rare; screenless normal: front | reject Bottled Flame/Lightning/Tornado and Whetstone; Dead Adventurer consumes its one shuffled token first; screenless generated output still consumes role member; its result commit is source-owned |
| Dead Adventurer fight reward | Only if constructed event combat contains unconsumed RELIC token; 0–49/50–82/83–99 Common/Uncommon/Rare; normal | Distinct from successful-search screenless offer. | Common/Uncommon/Rare; normal: front | only constructed event combat with unconsumed RELIC token; distinct from screenless search; separate generated offer consumes its own selected role member |
| Calling Bell continuation | One fixed Common, one fixed Uncommon, one fixed Rare offer after screen-down; screenless normal | Exactly three once-generated offers even if curse prevented; each has screenless filters and ordinary depletion/rejection/skip. | Common, Uncommon, Rare; screenless normal: front | three once-generated offers after screen-down, even when curse prevented; each applies screenless filters; each generated offer independently consumes its selected member; skip never restores |
| Merchant initial/restock stock | Rows 0/1: 0–47 Common, 48–81 Uncommon, 82–99 Rare; row 2 fixed Shop; end | In ShopRoom consume/reject Old Coin, Smiling Mask, MawBank, The Courier plus live `canSpawn`. Stock generation consumes regardless of later purchase/refusal; purchase/payment/restock is separate. | Common/Uncommon/Rare/Shop; end: back, including fixed Shop role | stock rows 0/1 tier roll, row 2 fixed Shop; ShopRoom rejects Old Coin/Smiling Mask/MawBank/The Courier and live canSpawn; stock generation consumes; later purchase/refusal/restock is separate; no rollback on declined offer |

#### Base member predicates and caller-local filters

The literal `canSpawn` exceptions below are live candidate predicates, not static removal from membership; every other listed ordinary candidate uses the accepted true base predicate. Rejected candidates remain role-local consumed history. Specific screenless/noncampfire/ShopRoom filters apply only at their named caller row above.

| Exact source candidate predicate | Accepted condition |
|---|---|
| Black Blood | Burning Blood |
| Ectoplasm | Act≤1 |
| Bottled Flame/Lightning/Tornado | respectively a nonbasic permanent Attack/nonbasic permanent Skill/permanent Power |
| Girya, Peace Pipe, Shovel | floor<48 and fewer than two owned among those three |
| Floor≤48 group | Ancient Tea Set, Ceramic Fish, Darkstone Periapt, Dream Catcher, Frozen Egg 2, Molten Egg 2, Toxic Egg 2, Juzu Bracelet, Meal Ticket, Meat on the Bone, Omamori, Potion Belt, Prayer Wheel, Question Card, Regal Pillow, Singing Bowl require floor≤48 |
| Matryoshka and WingedGreaves | floor≤40 |
| Tiny Chest | floor≤35 |
| PreservedInsect | floor≤52 |
| Merchant-only exclusions | Old Coin, Smiling Mask, MawBank, The Courier; four Shop exclusions also have their own floor≤48 predicate |

The five random role counts are Common 33, Uncommon 30, Rare 28, Boss 22, Shop 17 (130 pool identities: 120 shared ordinary plus ten Ironclad-red ordinary overlay identities already included). Add ten Special and one Starter to reach 141 authored identities; overlay is metadata, not extra membership. Circlet/Red Circlet are absent from these 141 authored IDs. The 35 display aliases resolve to one canonical constructor ID/key and never duplicate membership.

The native key namespace code `ContentNamespace.Relic=2` and existing pool/instance/offer records remain unchanged, but v12 changes `HistoryState.relics` from one `RelicHistory` to an exact ordered `Vec<RelicHistory>` of five role-keyed histories. This changes private core grammar, canonical core bytes, core schema identity/version, and replay header/file compatibility; it does not change observation/action wire identities, the descriptor preimage domain, or standalone combat-v1 pins. Full descriptor/fingerprint remains open. Exact downstream EXEC and whole relic-mechanics closure are not claimed. The v11 no-schema-change assertion was incorrect and is superseded by this v12 ruling.


### Relic role history schema correction (v12)

This section supersedes the v11 statements that one `RelicHistory` can represent each of five roles and that no core/replay version changes are required. The normative grammar in Appendix A is updated: `HistoryState.relics:Vec<RelicHistory>` is an exactly-five-element ordered vector. Its element order and `pool` values are exactly `OwnerKey(Relic,pool.common)`, `OwnerKey(Relic,pool.uncommon)`, `OwnerKey(Relic,pool.rare)`, `OwnerKey(Relic,pool.boss)`, `OwnerKey(Relic,pool.shop)`; pool keys are unique and no role is omitted, duplicated, unknown, or reordered. Each record independently owns `remaining_keys`, `consumed_rejected`, and `owned_exclusions`; vectors cannot be flattened, exchanged between roles, or inferred from one another. Every member belongs to exactly one role in the accepted closed relic registry. Each vector is duplicate-free; role-local categories are disjoint. `remaining_keys` is in ascending source pool ordinal and equals, in order, the role's unconsumed eligible `EconomyState.relic_pool` entries. `consumed_rejected` records rejected random selections in source order and agrees with the role's consumed pool state; generated offer IDs are tracked by the existing `EconomyState.consumed_ids`, never substituted for rejected history. `owned_exclusions` records the role-local initial-owned exclusion set and remains separate from both depletion categories. Acquired instances remain solely owned by `InventoryState.relics`; inventory ownership must agree with the pool/history exclusion or consumption cause, without introducing a copied owner authority. The five records and their order/vectors persist unchanged across Act2 carry.

Canonical bytes follow the existing `Vec<T>` and record grammar: U64 element count (exactly 5), then each `RelicHistory` in declared field order; each `Vec<OwnerKey>` is U64 count followed by its elements in semantic order. No serializer sorting or role flattening is permitted. Because the core grammar changes, the current root is `CoreStateV9=(version=9,run:RunState,private_catalog_revision:Hash32)`, canonical prefix is `A1CS || U16_BE(9) || SCHEMA("spire-lab-act1-core-candidate9",core_descriptor_hash)`, and the core schema reference identity is `spire-lab-act1-core-candidate9`. Replay schema identity is `spire-lab-act1-replay-v7`. The mandatory `ReplayHeaderV7.core_state` reference names the core identity and carries its descriptor hash; replay header and file grammars are `ReplayHeaderV7` and `ReplayFileV7` (file version 7). Old core-v8/replay-v6 records are incompatible and must reject rather than be interpreted under v9/v7. The private-core and replay component schemas and composed descriptor digest consequently change; the descriptor canonical preimage domain remains `spire-lab-act1-descriptor-v7`, because the observation descriptor grammar and action wire identity are unchanged. No final component, descriptor, or composed fingerprint is claimed before unresolved inputs close. Standalone `spire-lab-combat-observation-v1` and its accepted fingerprint remain unchanged.
